- **digital-twin:init**: Full first-time digital-twin run. Phases 1-6 + a JSON-extraction pass to produce PROFILE.html (card-styled), PROFILE.md, twin.md sub-agent, CLAUDE.md patch, gotchas, and canonical numbers. Wall-clock dominated by 2 LLM-bound phases; local Python pipeline runs in ~20 seconds. ([danielbentes/digital-twin](https://github.com/danielbentes/digital-twin/blob/HEAD/commands/init.md))
- **digital-twin:install-hook**: Install or uninstall the digital-twin pushback-detector as a Claude Code PostToolUse hook. Explicit, reversible, and incremental — never replays session history. ([danielbentes/digital-twin](https://github.com/danielbentes/digital-twin/blob/HEAD/commands/install-hook.md))
- **digital-twin:propose-rules**: Review pending memory rule and principle proposals from the digital-twin pushback detector. Approves or rejects each proposed correction before it lands in the user's memory. ([danielbentes/digital-twin](https://github.com/danielbentes/digital-twin/blob/HEAD/commands/propose-rules.md))
- **digital-twin:status**: Show the current state of the user's digital-twin artifacts — last-run timestamp, corpus size, drift since last run, twin agent install status. ([danielbentes/digital-twin](https://github.com/danielbentes/digital-twin/blob/HEAD/commands/status.md))
- **digital-twin:update**: Re-run the digital-twin pipeline against the latest logs, producing updated PROFILE.md, twin.md, and CLAUDE.md patch.
